<a id="p174"></a>

### P174 · A｜Do you think good service is important?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 倾听与清楚解释→解决关切、建立信任；换顾客场景。

**答案：**

> Yes, good service matters because customers want to feel that someone understands their problem.
> **Listening carefully and explaining things clearly help avoid misunderstandings and build trust.**
> For example, a shop assistant could listen to a customer's problem with a product, then explain how they can get it fixed.
> That said, being friendly isn't enough if the problem is left unresolved.
> Overall, good service means listening and providing useful help.

<a id="p175"></a>

### P175 · A｜Do the most expensive shops have the best service?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 服务看实际倾听与解决关切；价格不是充分证据，可核实评价。

**答案：**

> No, expensive shops don't necessarily have the best service.
> **What matters is whether the staff listen and give clear, helpful answers.**
> For example, an assistant in a small, affordable shop might explain a product patiently and answer all the customer's questions.
> That said, some expensive shops do offer excellent service.
> Overall, I'd judge the service by how customers are treated, not just the prices.

<a id="p176"></a>

### P176 · A｜Why should companies react quickly when customers have difficulties?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 及时回应→减少顾客不确定与误解→维持信任。

**答案：**

> Companies should reply quickly because customers can lose trust when they're left wondering what's happening.
> **A clear response reassures them that someone has heard their concern and is dealing with it.**
> For example, if a delivery is late, the company could reply straight away, explain the delay and say what happens next.
> So, a prompt, helpful reply can stop customers from losing confidence in the company.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

### P192 · A｜What should people do when members of a group have different opinions about a decision?

**串法：** 倾听分歧、表达理由、协调分工→共同决定。

**答案：**

> Group members should explain their ideas and listen to why others disagree.
> **Understanding each person's concerns** can help them find a plan they can all accept.
> For example, if a team disagrees about a deadline, they could discuss how long each task takes and agree on a workable date.
> So, listening and discussing the reasons can help a group reach a decision.

<a id="p209"></a>

### P209 · A｜What would you do if you haven't received a reply after sending out a message?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 礼貌追问、清楚说明急迫程度→减少误解；不用假定对方故意忽视。

**答案：**

> I'd send a polite follow-up, especially if I needed an answer soon.
> **Explaining when I need a reply** gives the other person a clearer idea of how urgent it is.
> For example, if I were waiting for a friend to confirm a booking, I'd ask whether they'd seen my message and mention the deadline.
> So, I'd check calmly rather than assume they were ignoring me.

<a id="p212"></a>

### P212 · A｜Why do some people feel angry when others don't reply to their message?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 没有回应→不确定/觉得不被理解→关系距离与负面情绪。

**答案：**

> Some people get angry because a missing reply makes them feel ignored.
> **Without a response, they may start to think the other person doesn't care about their concerns.**
> For example, someone who asks a close friend for support might feel hurt if they hear nothing back.
> So, the anger often comes from feeling that they don't matter to the other person.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

<a id="p214"></a>

### P214 · A｜What should a leader do to make team members want to follow him or her?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 倾听支持、明确分工→信任与稳定合作→愿意跟随。

**答案：**

> A leader should listen to the team, offer support and make everyone's responsibilities clear.
> **People are more willing to follow someone when they understand their role and feel their concerns matter.**
> For example, a project leader could ask what each person can take on, agree on the tasks and help anyone who's struggling.
> So, clear guidance and a willingness to help can earn the team's trust.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

<a id="p230"></a>

### P230 · A｜Who do young people like to share opinions with?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 选择愿意倾听和提供支持的人→理解与信任；可列朋友或家人。

**答案：**

> Young people often want to share opinions with someone who listens and understands their concerns.
> **That could be a friend, a parent or a teacher who has built a trusting relationship with them.**
> For example, a teenager may discuss a school problem with a friend who listens carefully before giving advice.
> So, young people often choose someone they trust and feel comfortable talking to.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p240"></a>

### P240 · A｜Would you tell others if you change your plan?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 及时清楚说明变化→少误解→便于共同协调。

**答案：**

> Yes, I'd tell anyone who's affected by the change.
> **That gives them time to adjust their own plans** and avoids confusion.
> For example, if I needed to change a meeting time, I'd message my friend and check whether the new time worked for them.
> I wouldn't need to tell people who weren't involved.
> So, I'd make sure everyone affected knew what had changed.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P240](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p240)。

<a id="p245"></a>

### P245 · A｜How would you tell your friends when you must change plans?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 清楚解释原因、更新安排→减少误解；用改见面时间例子。

**答案：**

> I'd send my friends a message explaining what had changed and why.
> **I'd also suggest another time and check whether it suited them**.
> For example, if I had to work late, I could ask whether we could meet for dinner the following evening instead.
> So, I'd explain the situation clearly and agree on a new plan with them.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P245](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p245)。

<a id="p250"></a>

### P250 · A｜Who can children turn to for help when making a decision?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)；辅助链：[AB14](#ab14)

**串法：** 找有经验且愿倾听的人，再核实信息→较好决定。

**答案：**

> Children can ask parents, teachers or other people who know them and have relevant experience.
> **Someone who listens and explains the options can help them check the facts and make a sensible choice.**
> For example, a child choosing an after-school activity could tell a teacher what they enjoy and check what each club actually involves.
> So, it helps to ask someone who understands both the child and the decision.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20a%20person%20did%20something%20to%20help%20you%20solve%20a%20problem.md) · [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P250](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p250)。

<a id="p292"></a>

### P292 · B｜What kinds of behavior are considered as good behavior?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB06 主链 / 对照](#ab06)

**串法：** 认真倾听、清楚回应和尊重他人 → 减少误解 → 建立信任。

**答案：**

> Listening carefully, responding respectfully and being clear with others are examples of good behaviour.
> **Taking another person’s concerns seriously** helps avoid misunderstandings and build trust.
> For example, someone can explain a delay politely instead of leaving a friend to guess what has happened.
> So, good behaviour includes thinking about others and treating them with respect.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P292](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p292)。

<a id="ab07"></a>

## AB07 共同相处与支持 → 关系和归属

**怎么串：** 拜访、聚会、社区和一起看比赛都说明共同活动怎样增进联系；只有人多或联系方便还不等于关系更好。

- **主链：** shared time and mutual support → emotional connection → stronger relationships
- **反向 / 对照：** limited interaction or constant distraction → emotional distance → weaker belonging

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P008](#p008) | A | 希望与人更亲近 → 更多共同活动和支持 → 追求受欢迎带来的联系感。 |
| [P061](#p061) | B | 与同学聊天和共餐 → 共享轻松时光 → 中学里值得回忆的快乐。 |
| [P065](#p065) | A | 共同活动、支持与联系→更好情绪；给一个共享活动例子。 |
| [P093](#p093) | B | 跨城仍联系并返乡见面 → 保持相处与支持 → 童年友谊继续。 |
| [P094](#p094) | A | 陪伴与支持→联系、归属和更好情绪。 |
| [P096](#p096) | A | 共享经历与支持形成真实联系→线上工具不能完全替代。 |
| [P112](#p112) | B | 与人群共同参与有兴趣的活动 → 分享情绪和互动 → 感到联系与快乐。 |
| [P120](#p120) | B | 以本人拜访朋友的素材作有限观察 → 喝咖啡聊天共享时间 → 部分人喜欢到家中拜访。 |
| [P121](#p121) | B | 聊天、喝茶咖啡或共餐 → 一起相处并听近况 → 拜访增进联系。 |
| [P124](#p124) | B | 带可分享的小礼物 → 为共同活动提供内容 → 帮助自然互动。 |
| [P125](#p125) | B | 每隔几个月拜访 Stella → 一起喝咖啡聊近况 → 在各自生活安排中维持联系。 |
| [P141](#p141) | B | 生日、重聚或重要事件 → 有理由聚在一起 → 共享时间强化联系。 |
| [P177](#p177) | A | 共享时间与支持→更强关系和归属。 |
| [P178](#p178) | B | 晚上聊天并分享书籍和生活 → 有倾听与支持 → 简单活动也愉快。 |
| [P180](#p180) | A | 保持交流、共同活动和互相支持→维持联系。 |
| [P229](#p229) | B | 在校与在家的实际相处机会不同 → 和老师或父母的交流量不同 → 不用关系价值证明频率。 |
| [P231](#p231) | A | 共同观看、分享感受→联系与情绪改善；不必引入竞技悬念链。 |
| [P232](#p232) | B | 体育场、家中等提供共同观看场景 → 分享感受 → 地点服务观赛互动。 |
| [P234](#p234) | B | 现场与观众同步互动，电视在家省出行 → 社交气氛与便利不同 → 明确两侧。 |
| [P235](#p235) | B | 用本人圈子的羽毛球兴趣和观赛例子 → 共同观看有参与感 → 不声称全国赛事第一。 |
| [P264](#p264) | B | 地方事件与共同生活有关 → 熟人会交流 → 用海岸清理故事限定范围。 |
| [P267](#p267) | A | 共同参与与互助→联系、归属和积极情绪。 |
| [P268](#p268) | B | 过去当面相处与现在远距联系方式不同 → 联系强弱取决于实际共同时间与支持 → 不断言过去必然更强。 |

<a id="p008"></a>

### P008 · A｜Why do people want to be popular?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 希望与人更亲近 → 更多共同活动和支持 → 追求受欢迎带来的联系感。

**答案：**

> I think many people want to be popular because they want to feel connected to others.
> **Spending time together and receiving support can give them a stronger sense of belonging.**
> For example, a student may want to be included in a friendly group that studies and spends weekends together.
> So, the wish to be popular can really be a wish for closer relationships.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

### P061 · B｜What was the happiest thing about your secondary school life?

**答案：**

> **Chatting and eating together helped us become close friends**.

> So, it's that everyday time with my classmates that I remember most fondly.

### P065 · A｜When do you think people are happiest?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**串法：** 共同活动、支持与联系→更好情绪；给一个共享活动例子。

**答案：**

> I think people are often happiest when they're spending time with people who care about them.
> **Doing things together and feeling supported can bring them closer and lift their mood.**
> For example, friends could go for a walk, talk through their worries and enjoy each other's company.
> So, feeling close to other people can be a big part of happiness.

<a id="p093"></a>

### P093 · B｜Do you still keep in touch with your friends from childhood? Why or why not?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 跨城仍联系并返乡见面 → 保持相处与支持 → 童年友谊继续。

**答案：**

> Yes, I still keep in touch with my childhood friend Ming.
> **We still support each other**, which helps us stay close even though we live in different cities.
> For example, we could catch up during a visit home and talk about anything that's been worrying us.
> We don't need to message every day to stay friends.
> So, I keep in touch because the friendship still means a lot to me.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

<a id="p094"></a>

### P094 · A｜How important is childhood friendship to children?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 陪伴与支持→联系、归属和更好情绪。

**答案：**

> Childhood friendships are important because they give children company and support.
> **Spending time with friends helps them build trust and feel that they belong.**
> For example, a child who's worried at school might feel better after talking and playing with a close friend.
> That said, a supportive friendship matters more than simply a long-lasting one.
> Overall, good friends can make childhood a happier experience.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p096"></a>

### P096 · A｜Do you think online communication through social media will replace face-to-face communication?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 共享经历与支持形成真实联系→线上工具不能完全替代。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think social media will completely replace face-to-face communication.
> **Relationships also grow through doing things together and being there for each other in person.**
> For example, spending an afternoon with a friend who's having a hard time can feel more supportive than exchanging a few short messages.
> That said, online messages are still useful for keeping in touch.
> Overall, I think people will continue to want both.

### P112 · B｜Why do some people enjoy being in crowded places?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**答案：**

> Some people enjoy crowded places when they can share an activity with the people around them.
> **Reacting together can create a sense of connection** rather than just feeling surrounded by strangers.
> For example, spectators at a badminton final may cheer the same point and enjoy the excitement together.
> So, a crowd can be enjoyable when it is part of a shared experience.

> That said, some people prefer meeting in a café or restaurant.
> Overall, home visits appeal to people who enjoy a relaxed catch-up.

> **They have more chances to talk to teachers during the school day and to parents when they're at home.**

> So, there's no single answer for every child; their daily routine makes a difference.

> So, watching sport can be an enjoyable way to connect with others.

### P234 · B｜What are the main differences between watching a sports event live in a stadium and watching it on TV?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB13](#ab13)

**串法：** 现场与观众同步互动，电视在家省出行 → 社交气氛与便利不同 → 明确两侧。

**答案：**

> Watching in a stadium lets you feel the crowd's excitement, while watching on TV is more convenient.
> **At the stadium, you cheer along with other fans; at home, you can enjoy the match without travelling**.
> For example, a badminton final might feel more exciting in a packed venue, but someone watching at home can avoid the journey back afterwards.
> So, it's a choice between the live atmosphere and the comfort of staying home.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**答案：**

> Badminton matches are popular to watch among people I know in China.
> **Watching together lets fans share the excitement and talk about the players**.
> For example, friends could watch a final together and discuss how the winner came back after losing the first game.
> So, badminton is one example I'd give, though I wouldn't say it's everyone's favourite.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a live sport match you have ever watched](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕Describe%20a%20live%20sport%20match%20you%20have%20ever%20watched.md) · [Describe a popular person](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20popular%20person.md)

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E48](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#48-balanced-routines-and-social-connection-均衡作息与人际联系) · [审计 P235](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p235)。

<a id="p264"></a>

### P264 · B｜Are there a lot of people talking about local news?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 地方事件与共同生活有关 → 熟人会交流 → 用海岸清理故事限定范围。

**答案：**

> Local news can get people talking, especially when it concerns a place they know.
> **A story feels more relevant when it's connected to their own community.**
> For example, news about a beach clean-up could get nearby residents talking about keeping the beach tidy.
> So, it can start conversations locally, although I couldn't say how many people follow it.

**学习提示：** 沿用地方新闻练习，不把“引起关注”扩写为本轮核实的统计。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md)

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P264](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p264)。

<a id="p267"></a>

### P267 · A｜Why do some people want to be very involved in their community?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 共同参与与互助→联系、归属和积极情绪。

**答案：**

> Some people get involved in their community because they want to feel closer to the people around them.
> **Doing things together and helping one another give them a sense of belonging.**
> For example, neighbours could organise a regular walk where they chat and listen to anyone who's had a difficult week.
> So, taking part can help people feel more connected and supported.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a friend from your childhood](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md) · [Describe an enjoyable evening with your friends](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20an%20enjoyable%20evening%20with%20your%20friends.md)

论点回查：[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系)、[E48](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#48-balanced-routines-and-social-connection-均衡作息与人际联系) · [审计 P267](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p267)。

<a id="p268"></a>

### P268 · B｜Do you think local communities were stronger in the past than they are today?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB07 主链 / 对照](#ab07)

**串法：** 过去当面相处与现在远距联系方式不同 → 联系强弱取决于实际共同时间与支持 → 不断言过去必然更强。

**答案：**

> I don't think local communities were necessarily stronger in the past.
> **What matters is whether neighbours spend time together and help each other**, even if they now arrange it differently.
> For example, neighbours might once have arranged a clean-up by talking in the street, while now they could use a group chat.
> So, communities can still be close today if people stay involved.

**学习提示：** 过去/现在为常识性方式对照，不声称全国社区关系变化调查。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md) · [Describe a friend from your childhood](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P268](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p268)。

<a id="ab08"></a>

## AB08 明确分工与互助 → 合作结果

**怎么串：** 把软件项目里的分工、检查与遗漏迁移到学校或服务场景。原因题、解决题、领导判断题都落在职责是否清楚。

- **主链：** clear roles and mutual support → stable cooperation → stronger collective results
- **反向 / 对照：** unclear responsibility or reluctance to communicate → coordination problems → weaker teamwork

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P017](#p017) | A | 团队运动→分工合作，或规律运动→健康习惯；用球类训练例子。 |
| [P173](#p173) | B | 店员职责不清 → 重复处理或遗漏需求 → 顾客收到差服务。 |
| [P213](#p213) | A | 重复分工和沟通协调问题→降低效率；独立任务可避免这类成本，限定任务适合独做。 |
| [P215](#p215) | A | 学分工与支持→协调顺畅→集体结果更好。 |
| [P216](#p216) | B | 小组展示和制作项目 → 按任务分工并互相衔接 → 学校中的合作任务。 |
| [P217](#p217) | A | 实践分工与沟通→合作技能与集体表现。 |
| [P218](#p218) | A | 看是否倾听、明确职责和维持协调→团队实际结果。 |
| [P236](#p236) | B | 规则易理解且能看到配合的比赛 → 观察分工与互助 → 适合儿童理解合作。 |

<a id="p017"></a>

### P017 · A｜What can children learn from doing sports?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 团队运动→分工合作，或规律运动→健康习惯；用球类训练例子。

**答案：**

> Children can learn teamwork and build healthy habits through sport.
> **They get used to playing their part and supporting others, while regular training makes exercise part of their routine.**
> For example, children who attend football practice each week can learn to cover different positions and help their teammates during a match.
> So, sport can teach them to work with others and stay active.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P017](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p017)。

<a id="p173"></a>

### P173 · B｜Why is customer service not good in some shops?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 店员职责不清 → 重复处理或遗漏需求 → 顾客收到差服务。

**答案：**

> Customer service can be poor when staff responsibilities are unclear.
> **Not knowing who should handle a request** can lead to delays or leave a customer’s problem unanswered.
> For example, two assistants might each assume the other is checking a laptop warranty, so neither gives the customer a reply.
> So, service can suffer when nobody knows who should deal with a problem.

**学习提示：** 将小组重复与遗漏任务迁移到店员场景，不增写“店员缺培训”等独立原因。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md)

论点回查：[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P173](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p173)。

<a id="p213"></a>

### P213 · A｜Why do some people prefer to work by themselves?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 重复分工和沟通协调问题→降低效率；独立任务可避免这类成本，限定任务适合独做。

**答案：**

> Some people prefer working alone because organising work with others can be frustrating.
> **If responsibilities aren't clear, tasks can get repeated or delayed.**
> For example, two people might both write the same section of a report because neither knows who's meant to do it.
> So, some people find working alone simpler when the task allows it.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E36](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#36-why-are-clear-roles-important-in-teamwork-明确分工的重要性)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P213](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p213)。

<a id="p215"></a>

### P215 · A｜Should students learn to do group work?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 学分工与支持→协调顺畅→集体结果更好。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think students should learn to work in groups.
> **Sharing tasks and helping one another teach them how to get things done together.**
> For example, students doing a project could divide up the research, writing and presentation, then help a classmate who's falling behind.
> That said, each person needs a clear role so work isn't repeated or missed.
> Overall, group work gives students useful practice in cooperating.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P215](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p215)。

<a id="p216"></a>

### P216 · B｜What group tasks are there in schools?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 小组展示和制作项目 → 按任务分工并互相衔接 → 学校中的合作任务。

**答案：**

> Schools can give students group presentations, model-building tasks or shared research projects.
> **Clear roles help them combine their work** instead of repeating the same part.
> For example, a presentation group could divide research, slide preparation and speaking, then check how the parts fit together.
> So, these tasks give students a chance to work towards one goal together.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P216](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p216)。

<a id="p217"></a>

### P217 · A｜What advantages are there for students experiencing teamwork at school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 实践分工与沟通→合作技能与集体表现。

**答案：**

> Teamwork at school helps students learn to share responsibilities and communicate.
> **They practise fitting their work together and helping classmates reach the same goal.**
> For example, a presentation group could divide the sections, check that the ideas connect and help anyone who needs more practice.
> That said, everyone needs a clear role for the group to work well.
> Overall, these tasks can help students become better team members.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P217](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p217)。

<a id="p218"></a>

### P218 · A｜How can you tell if a person is a good leader?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 看是否倾听、明确职责和维持协调→团队实际结果。

**答案：**

> I'd look at whether they give clear guidance and listen to the team.
> **A good leader makes sure everyone knows their role and can work together without confusion.**
> For example, if two people are doing the same task, the leader could hear both sides and help them agree on who should do what.
> So, I'd judge them by how well they organise and support the team.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E29](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#29-what-does-effective-communication-involve-有效沟通)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E37](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#37-what-happens-when-responsibilities-arent-clear-职责不清的后果) · [审计 P218](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p218)。

<a id="p236"></a>

### P236 · B｜What types of sports matches are suitable for children to watch?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T03 沟通、关系与合作 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB08 主链 / 对照](#ab08)

**串法：** 规则易理解且能看到配合的比赛 → 观察分工与互助 → 适合儿童理解合作。

**答案：**

> Team sports with rules children can follow are a good choice.
> **Seeing how players work together** gives them something useful to notice beyond the score.
> For example, a doubles badminton match can show how two players cover the court and support each other.
> So, I’d choose a match children can follow and discuss afterwards.

**学习提示：** 从羽毛球观赛迁移为双打示例，不声称已有素材那场决赛是双打。

论点回查：[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P236](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p236)。

<a id="ab09"></a>

## AB09 扩大资源获取 → 更多学习机会

**怎么串：** 说明谁原本不便获得资源、怎样获得机会。线上学习只是渠道，核心是原先的获取障碍减少。

- **主链：** wider access to basic resources → narrower opportunity gaps → greater social mobility
- **反向 / 对照：** unequal access → fewer opportunities → entrenched disadvantage

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P299](#p299) | A | 扩大教育获取机会、节省时间→学习更便利；已有线上课程语境。 |

<a id="p299"></a>

### P299 · A｜What are the advantages of online learning?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB09 主链 / 对照](#ab09)；辅助链：[AB13](#ab13)

**串法：** 扩大教育获取机会、节省时间→学习更便利；已有线上课程语境。

**答案：**

> Online learning gives people access to more courses and saves them travel time.
> **They can learn from a teacher who's far away without having to leave home.**
> For example, someone who lives far from a college could take one of its online classes instead of spending hours getting there and back.
> That said, they still need to put in the effort and practise.
> Overall, it can make learning easier to fit into people's lives.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md) · [Describe an important decision that you made](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md)

论点回查：[E17](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#17-can-online-education-reduce-inequality)、[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖) · [审计 P299](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p299)。

<a id="ab10"></a>

## AB10 清晰公平的规则 → 可预测行为与责任

**怎么串：** 正向解释规则和后果，反向解释过度控制。具体校规可作假设，实际法规的名称和实施状态不能从建议中生成。

- **主链：** clear and fair rules → predictable consequences → more responsible behaviour
- **反向 / 对照：** excessive or inconsistent rules → resistance and unfairness → weaker trust

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P063](#p063) | A | 可主张不必监控所有活动：过度控制→抵触与不信任；倾听支持可替代全面监控。自主权链可深化但不是必需。 |
| [P273](#p273) | A | 可用过度控制→抵触与信任下降，倾听建议→支持来直接回答；自主选择→责任链是可补深化，不是本题必需前提。 |
| [P290](#p290) | B | 按约完成任务、尊重发言和公共物品 → 行为可预测 → 学校合作更顺畅。 |
| [P291](#p291) | B | 实际守法率不能由规则价值推断 → 只表达有限看法 → 例子说明规则如何指导行为。 |
| [P293](#p293) | B | 家人或生活场景解释规则 → 理解要求和后果 → 学校外也能学习法律意识。 |
| [P294](#p294) | A | 可预测行为与明确责任→信任、合作顺畅。 |
| [P295](#p295) | A | 解释清楚规则、保持一致后果→孩子理解并承担责任。 |
| [P303](#p303) | B | 借小组任务的清晰要求 → 列按时完成、尊重同伴等示例 → 不声称全国统一校规。 |

<a id="p063"></a>

### P063 · A｜Do you think teachers should always know what their students are doing?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 可主张不必监控所有活动：过度控制→抵触与不信任；倾听支持可替代全面监控。自主权链可深化但不是必需。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think teachers need to know everything their students are doing.
> **Being watched too closely can make students feel they're being treated unfairly and lose trust in the teacher.**
> For example, a student who's questioned about every after-school activity might become less willing to talk openly.
> That said, teachers should still be there when students need support.
> Overall, listening and offering help are better than trying to monitor everything.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P063](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p063)。

<a id="p273"></a>

### P273 · A｜Should parents interfere with their children's ambitions?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)；辅助链：[AB06](#ab06)

**串法：** 可用过度控制→抵触与信任下降，倾听建议→支持来直接回答；自主选择→责任链是可补深化，不是本题必需前提。

**答案：**

> Parents should offer advice, but I don't think they should control their children's ambitions.
> **Pushing too hard can make children resist and become less willing to trust them.**
> For example, if parents keep dismissing a child's interest in art without listening, the child may stop discussing future plans with them.
> That said, parents can still explain their concerns calmly.
> Overall, it's better to listen and guide than to take over.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P273](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p273)。

<a id="p290"></a>

### P290 · B｜What rules should students follow at school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 按约完成任务、尊重发言和公共物品 → 行为可预测 → 学校合作更顺畅。

**答案：**

> Students should hand in work on time, respect others and take care of school property.
> **These rules help them understand what they're responsible for** and avoid causing problems for classmates.
> For example, returning shared equipment after a lesson means the next class can use it too.
> So, school rules help everyone learn and share the space more smoothly.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P290](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p290)。

<a id="p291"></a>

### P291 · B｜Do people in your country usually obey the law?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 实际守法率不能由规则价值推断 → 只表达有限看法 → 例子说明规则如何指导行为。

**答案：**

> I think many people in China try to obey the law, but I can't judge how consistently everyone does.
> **Clear rules and knowing the consequences of breaking them can encourage people to follow them.**
> For example, a clearly explained rule about disposing of rubbish, including what happens if it's ignored, could help people follow it.
> Of course, some may still choose to ignore the rule.
> So, my impression is that many people try to comply, but behaviour varies.

**学习提示：** Part 2 的环保法是提议；这里的规则例子是假设，不称其已经实施。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects) · [审计 P291](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p291)。

<a id="p293"></a>

### P293 · B｜Do you think children can learn about the law outside of school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 家人或生活场景解释规则 → 理解要求和后果 → 学校外也能学习法律意识。

**答案：**

> Yes, children can learn about the law from their parents and everyday situations.
> **Simple explanations help them understand what's allowed and how their actions affect others.**
> For example, a parent could explain that damaging someone's property is against the law and may mean paying for repairs.
> The explanation needs to suit the child's age.
> So, children don't have to be in a classroom to start learning about the law.

**学习提示：** 不把拟议海岸塑料禁令当现行法；具体法名仍需真实材料。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E30](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#30-example-of-active-listening-积极倾听的例子) · [审计 P293](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p293)。

<a id="p294"></a>

### P294 · A｜What are the benefits for people if they obey the rules?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)；辅助链：[AB08](#ab08)

**串法：** 可预测行为与明确责任→信任、合作顺畅。

**答案：**

> Following clear, fair rules can make everyday cooperation easier.
> **When everyone knows what to expect, it's easier to plan and work together.**
> For example, students following an agreed schedule for group work know when each person should finish their part.
> That said, rules need to be fair and applied consistently.
> Overall, sensible rules can help people work together more smoothly.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E35](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#35-what-does-effective-teamwork-mean-有效团队合作) · [审计 P294](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p294)。

<a id="p295"></a>

### P295 · A｜How can parents teach children to obey rules?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 解释清楚规则、保持一致后果→孩子理解并承担责任。

**答案：**

> Parents can explain rules clearly and respond consistently when children follow or break them.
> **Knowing what will happen makes the rules easier to understand and take seriously.**
> For example, a parent could explain that toys must be put away before another game starts and stick to that rule each time.
> So, clear expectations and a consistent response can help children learn to follow rules.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md) · [Describe something you do regularly that you think is a waste of time](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20something%20you%20do%20regularly%20that%20you%20think%20is%20a%20waste%20of%20time.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility)、[E20](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#20-can-excessive-or-inconsistent-rules-have-negative-effects) · [审计 P295](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p295)。

<a id="p303"></a>

### P303 · B｜What kind of rules do schools in China have?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB10 主链 / 对照](#ab10)

**串法：** 借小组任务的清晰要求 → 列按时完成、尊重同伴等示例 → 不声称全国统一校规。

**答案：**

> Common school rules in China include handing in homework on time and respecting teachers and classmates.
> **Clear rules help students know what's expected of them**.
> For example, a school might ask students to listen while someone else is speaking so everyone gets a turn.
> So, the details vary, but many rules are about helping everyone learn together.

**学习提示：** Part 2 没有具体学校校规；例子是示意规则，不冒充全国统一规定或本人学校事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E19](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#19-can-rules-teach-children-responsibility) · [审计 P303](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p303)。

<a id="ab11"></a>

## AB11 针对性公共投入 → 更好的服务

**怎么串：** 把钱如何形成服务和公共收益说清楚。研究资金必须有研究结果到支持的桥梁，不直接等同日常医疗服务。

- **主链：** targeted spending → better essential services → wider public benefit
- **反向 / 对照：** high costs or poor allocation → financial pressure → less funding for other needs

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P069](#p069) | B | 研究投入 → 改善发现和应对健康问题的方法 → 潜在公共收益；仍需比较其他服务需求。 |

<a id="p069"></a>

### P069 · B｜Do you think governments should put a large amount of money into medical research?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T04 公平、规则与公共资源 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB11 主链 / 对照](#ab11)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 研究投入 → 改善发现和应对健康问题的方法 → 潜在公共收益；仍需比较其他服务需求。

**答案：**

> I think governments should spend a reasonable amount on medical research.
> **Finding better ways to detect and treat illness** could improve the care people receive.
> For example, research might help doctors spot an illness earlier, so patients can get help sooner.
> That said, hospitals and other public services need funding too.
> So, research deserves support, but the spending needs to be balanced.

**学习提示：** 这是投入评价；例子是研究到服务的潜在路径，不声称某项研究已成功。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who would like to choose a career in the medical field (e.g. a doctor, a nurse)](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20would%20like%20to%20choose%20a%20career%20in%20the%20medical%20field%20%28e.g.%20a%20doctor%2C%20a%20nurse%29.md)

论点回查：[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money)、[E57](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#57-how-can-governments-support-peoples-healthwell-being) · [审计 P069](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p069)。

<a id="ab12"></a>

## AB12 满足需求 → 企业收入与岗位

**怎么串：** 软件企业、旅游和消费换具体产品或服务；先说谁需要它，再接收入或就业。

- **主链：** useful products or services → business revenue and jobs → economic growth
- **反向 / 对照：** unstable demand or job loss → income pressure → wider inequality

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P032](#p032) | A | 供给不符合实际需求→需求不稳、收入受影响；据此解释经营有难度。 |
| [P034](#p034) | A | 可用创造实际价值、保持健康作为成功标准；金钱不是这些结果的充分条件。 |
| [P046](#p046) | A | 传统产品→销售收入与岗位，并保留文化价值；不夸大整个产业占比。 |
| [P087](#p087) | A | 出售有用产品或服务→收入与就业；可把获得收入作为创业动机。 |
| [P088](#p088) | B | 创业项目满足真实需求 → 资金帮助提供服务和岗位 → 可支持，但应核对用途和机会成本。 |
| [P090](#p090) | A | 满足真实需求→稳定销售收入→经营成功。 |
| [P091](#p091) | A | 产品不符合需求→需求不稳→经营失败。 |
| [P113](#p113) | B | 城市发展增加服务需求 → 企业收入和岗位增加；公共设施也承受压力 → 评价有条件。 |
| [P131](#p131) | A | 游客购买服务→商家收入与就业→地方经济受益。 |
| [P133](#p133) | A | 旅游服务收入→就业→经济增长；用住宿餐饮例子。 |
| [P172](#p172) | A | 购买有用产品→收入与岗位→经济增长。 |
| [P276](#p276) | B | 部分更高职位提供实际收入或发展空间 → 满足生活和职业需要 → 愿意争取。 |

<a id="p032"></a>

### P032 · A｜Is it difficult to run a business in today’s world?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 供给不符合实际需求→需求不稳、收入受影响；据此解释经营有难度。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think running a business can be difficult.
> **Businesses need to sell things people want, or they won't earn enough to keep going.**
> For example, a shop selling products that local customers rarely need may struggle to make enough sales.
> That said, a business meeting a clear need has a better chance.
> Overall, keeping customers interested is a major challenge for businesses.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P032](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p032)。

<a id="p034"></a>

### P034 · A｜Does having money necessarily mean being successful?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB04](#ab04)

**串法：** 可用创造实际价值、保持健康作为成功标准；金钱不是这些结果的充分条件。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think having money necessarily makes someone successful.
> **Doing something useful and staying healthy can also be signs of success.**
> For example, someone could run a small repair business that helps local people and still have time to look after their health, without becoming rich.
> That said, a steady income still matters.
> Overall, I'd look at more than someone's bank balance.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P034](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p034)。

<a id="p046"></a>

### P046 · A｜How important are traditional handicrafts to a country's industry?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB19](#ab19)

**串法：** 传统产品→销售收入与岗位，并保留文化价值；不夸大整个产业占比。

**答案：**

> Traditional handicrafts can be important, especially for local businesses.
> **Selling handmade goods provides income and jobs while keeping traditional skills in use.**
> For example, a basket-making workshop could sell its products and employ local craftspeople to make them.
> That said, handicrafts are only one part of a country's industry.
> Overall, they can support local jobs and help preserve traditional skills.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P046](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p046)。

<a id="p087"></a>

### P087 · A｜Why do some people start their own business?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 出售有用产品或服务→收入与就业；可把获得收入作为创业动机。

**答案：**

> Some people start a business because they see a way to earn a living by meeting a need.
> **If people find the product or service useful, they're more likely to pay for it.**
> For example, someone who's good at repairs could charge local residents to fix broken household items.
> So, turning a useful skill into an income can be a reason to start.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P087](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p087)。

<a id="p088"></a>

### P088 · B｜Should governments provide financial support to start-ups?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB11](#ab11)

**串法：** 创业项目满足真实需求 → 资金帮助提供服务和岗位 → 可支持，但应核对用途和机会成本。

**答案：**

> I think governments should offer some support to start-ups with a useful idea and a realistic plan.
> **Funding can help them get a product or service ready to sell and may allow them to employ people.**
> For example, a small software team could use the money to hire a developer and test a tool that local firms need for managing records.
> That said, that money also needs to be weighed against other public needs.
> Overall, support makes sense when there's a clear use for the funding.

**学习提示：** 借用 Chen 的小企业软件服务场景；资助是建议，不声称他的企业实际获资助。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P088](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p088)。

<a id="p090"></a>

### P090 · A｜What makes a business successful?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 满足真实需求→稳定销售收入→经营成功。

**答案：**

> A business is more likely to succeed when it provides something people actually need.
> **Useful products and services give customers a reason to buy, which helps the business earn money.**
> For example, a reliable local cleaning service can earn money by helping residents with a task they need done.
> So, a business needs to give customers a good reason to keep coming back.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P090](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p090)。

<a id="p091"></a>

### P091 · A｜What makes a business fail?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 产品不符合需求→需求不稳→经营失败。

**答案：**

> A business can fail when its products or services do not match what people need.
> **If too few people buy what it sells, it may not earn enough to stay open.**
> For example, a shop may keep stocking products that very few of its customers want to buy.
> So, losing touch with customers' needs can put a business at risk.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p113"></a>

### P113 · B｜Do you think the continued growth of large cities is a positive trend?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB11](#ab11)

**串法：** 城市发展增加服务需求 → 企业收入和岗位增加；公共设施也承受压力 → 评价有条件。

**答案：**

> The growth of large cities can be positive because it can create more business and job opportunities.
> **More residents mean more demand for goods and services, which can help businesses expand.**
> For example, a growing city could have more local firms needing software support, allowing a small IT company to hire extra staff.
> However, schools, transport and other services may struggle to keep up.
> Overall, the benefits are greater when public services can cope with the growth.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md) · [Describe a crowded place you went to](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20crowded%20place%20you%20went%20to.md)

<a id="p131"></a>

### P131 · A｜Why do many countries try to attract people to visit?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 游客购买服务→商家收入与就业→地方经济受益。

**答案：**

> Many countries try to attract visitors because tourism brings money into local businesses and supports jobs.
> **Visitors spend on places to stay, meals and transport, giving those businesses more customers.**
> For example, a busy guesthouse could earn more from visitors and hire local people to help run it.
> So, tourism can be a useful source of income and work.

<a id="p133"></a>

### P133 · A｜What are the benefits for cities of having lots of tourists?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 旅游服务收入→就业→经济增长；用住宿餐饮例子。

**答案：**

> Lots of tourists can bring a city more income and jobs.
> **Visitors spend money in local businesses, which helps those businesses keep people in work.**
> For example, a restaurant near a popular attraction could serve tourists and employ local residents as cooks and waiters.
> That said, visitor numbers can go up and down.
> Overall, tourism can help the local economy.

<a id="p172"></a>

### P172 · A｜Can consumption drive economic growth?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)；辅助链：[AB17](#ab17)

**串法：** 购买有用产品→收入与岗位→经济增长。

**答案：**

> Yes, consumer spending can help the economy grow.
> **When people pay for goods and services, businesses earn money and can keep people in work.**
> For example, residents who pay a local cleaning company give it an income and help provide jobs for cleaners.
> That said, buying things we don't need can create waste.
> Overall, spending on useful goods and services can support businesses and jobs.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p276"></a>

### P276 · B｜Why are young people ambitious for higher positions?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T05 经济与就业 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB12 主链 / 对照](#ab12)

**串法：** 部分更高职位提供实际收入或发展空间 → 满足生活和职业需要 → 愿意争取。

**答案：**

> Some young people aim for higher positions because they want better pay or a chance to develop their skills.
> **Those benefits can help them meet their financial needs and move towards their career goals.**
> For example, a developer might seek a senior role that pays more and lets them take responsibility for a larger project.
> That said, a higher position doesn't always mean a better working life.
> So, it's often the opportunities that come with the role that appeal to them.

**学习提示：** 例子明确这份角色具有待遇和项目条件，不声称所有晋升都如此。

论点回查：[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P276](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p276)。

<a id="ab13"></a>

## AB13 减少时间精力 → 便利；过度代劳 → 依赖

**怎么串：** 比较线上线下时给两侧；涉及 AI 学习时保留主动尝试。不能把“科技发达”本身当作原因。

- **主链：** save time and effort → complete tasks more easily → improve efficiency
- **反向 / 对照：** excessive convenience → put in less active effort → greater dependence

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P048](#p048) | B | 过去手工处理部分重复步骤 → 现在工具可辅助 → 节省时间；手艺人的判断仍需要保留。 |
| [P058](#p058) | B | 过去联系受距离限制时较不方便 → 现在远程交流可减少等待与奔波 → 只评价便利的这一面。 |
| [P095](#p095) | A | 远程消息减少联系障碍 → 易于分享近况 → 有利维持联系；过度使用也可能挤占当面相处。 |
| [P098](#p098) | A | 跨城即时交换消息 → 相比等待见面更易保持联系 → 友谊维持方式改变。 |
| [P170](#p170) | B | 线上免出行节省时间，线下可亲自检查 → 便利与信息获取不同 → 根据购买需要选择。 |
| [P171](#p171) | A | 线上省时→效率；信息不可靠或数据滥用→错误选择/不信任，选择一组利弊。 |
| [P201](#p201) | A | 省时高效与减少主动投入→依赖，已练完整利弊。 |
| [P202](#p202) | A | 学会使用工具→效率；保留独立练习→避免依赖。 |
| [P203](#p203) | A | 适度使用提高效率，独立练习避免能力弱化；用儿童学习例子。 |
| [P204](#p204) | A | 自动化省时省力→任务更高效；软件开发例子可直接使用。 |
| [P205](#p205) | A | 过度便利→减少主动努力→依赖与独立思考弱化；限定部分学生。 |
| [P233](#p233) | A | 快捷获取赛事、省时省力→观看方便；换观看场景即可。 |
| [P255](#p255) | B | 在家观看省出行，影院提供专门设施 → 便利与观影环境不同 → 双侧比较。 |
| [P309](#p309) | B | 家庭传统延续，而当前日常也可选择方便的外卖 → 获取方式改变但并非旧饮食全消失。 |
| [P310](#p310) | B | 外出就餐省准备，家中一起做饭有共享过程 → 不同场合偏好不同。 |

<a id="p048"></a>

### P048 · B｜How does modern technology change the handicraft industry?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)

**串法：** 过去手工处理部分重复步骤 → 现在工具可辅助 → 节省时间；手艺人的判断仍需要保留。

**答案：**

> Modern technology can make some parts of handicraft work quicker than before.
> **Tools can help with repeated preparation tasks**, leaving makers more time for the parts that need their own judgement.
> For example, a craftsperson might prepare a repeated pattern digitally instead of drawing every copy from scratch.
> So, technology can speed up some jobs while leaving others to the craftsperson.

**学习提示：** 工具仅作常识性示意，不声称某行业普遍采用或完全机械化。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who likes to make things by hand (e.g. toys, furniture)](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20likes%20to%20make%20things%20by%20hand%20%28e.g.%20toys%2C%20furniture%29.md) · [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P048](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p048)。

<a id="p058"></a>

### P058 · B｜Has the quality of life for old people improved compared to the past?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 过去联系受距离限制时较不方便 → 现在远程交流可减少等待与奔波 → 只评价便利的这一面。

**答案：**

> In some ways, yes, especially when it comes to staying in touch with family.
> **Older people can now contact relatives quickly**, instead of waiting for a visit to ask for help.
> For example, a parent in Zhanjiang could call their child in Beijing to talk through a problem.
> That said, not every older person finds these tools easy to use.
> So, easier communication can improve daily life, though it isn't the whole picture.

**学习提示：** 借用异地家庭与联系素材作有限比较；没有使用网页上的养老服务数据。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a friend from your childhood](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20friend%20from%20your%20childhood.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money)、[E57](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#57-how-can-governments-support-peoples-healthwell-being) · [审计 P058](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p058)。

<a id="p095"></a>

### P095 · A｜What do you think of communicating with others through social media?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 远程消息减少联系障碍 → 易于分享近况 → 有利维持联系；过度使用也可能挤占当面相处。

**答案：**

> I think social media is a useful way to keep in touch.
> **It allows people to exchange messages quickly even when they cannot meet in person.**
> For example, friends living in different cities can share updates and arrange their next visit through a group chat.
> However, constant phone use can reduce the time they spend interacting face to face.
> Overall, social media works best when it helps people stay close.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P095](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p095)。

<a id="p098"></a>

### P098 · A｜Has technology changed people's friendships? How?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 跨城即时交换消息 → 相比等待见面更易保持联系 → 友谊维持方式改变。

**答案：**

> Yes, technology has made it easier for friends to stay in touch when they live far apart.
> **They can share everyday news straight away**, instead of waiting until they meet again.
> For example, friends in different cities could send each other messages after work and arrange their next visit.
> However, checking phones constantly can get in the way when friends are actually together.
> So, technology helps people stay connected, but it can also distract them.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a challenging technological problem you faced](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20challenging%20technological%20problem%20you%20faced.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P098](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p098)。

<a id="p170"></a>

### P170 · B｜What are the differences between online shopping and in-store shopping?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB14](#ab14)

**串法：** 线上免出行节省时间，线下可亲自检查 → 便利与信息获取不同 → 根据购买需要选择。

**答案：**

> Online shopping saves you a trip, while shopping in a store lets you check the product yourself.
> **You can order from home online, but in a shop you can see and try what you're buying**.
> For example, ordering a laptop online is convenient, while visiting a store lets you test the keyboard first.
> So, one saves time, and the other gives you a better feel for the product.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P170](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p170)。

<a id="p171"></a>

### P171 · A｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping online?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB14](#ab14)

**串法：** 线上省时→效率；信息不可靠或数据滥用→错误选择/不信任，选择一组利弊。

**答案：**

> Online shopping can save people time and effort, but the information they see is not always reliable.
> **They can complete a purchase easily without having to visit a shop.**
> For example, someone can order a household item from home instead of making a separate shopping trip.
> However, an exaggerated product description may lead them to buy something unsuitable.
> Overall, online shopping is convenient, but it requires careful judgement.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a challenging technological problem you faced](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20challenging%20technological%20problem%20you%20faced.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E52](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#52-how-can-technology-affect-peoples-privacytrust)、[E53](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#53-technologyprivacy--developed-version) · [审计 P171](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p171)。

<a id="p201"></a>

### P201 · A｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)

**串法：** 省时高效与减少主动投入→依赖，已练完整利弊。

**答案：**

> AI can save time and effort, but people may start relying on it too much.
> **It can handle routine parts of a task, so people have less work to do themselves.**
> For example, a software developer can use AI to help with a routine coding task and finish it sooner.
> However, relying on it for every step can reduce independent practice and weaken skills.
> So, AI is helpful as long as people still do some thinking for themselves.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a challenging technological problem you faced](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20challenging%20technological%20problem%20you%20faced.md) · [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md)

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖) · [审计 P201](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p201)。

<a id="p202"></a>

### P202 · A｜Do you think people today should learn about AI technology?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 学会使用工具→效率；保留独立练习→避免依赖。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think people should learn enough about AI to use it sensibly.
> **Learning how to work with these tools can help them complete tasks more efficiently.**
> For example, an employee can practise using an AI tool to help organise a first draft while reviewing the result themselves.
> That said, people still need to develop their own skills.
> Overall, learning about AI can be useful alongside independent practice.

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P202](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p202)。

<a id="p203"></a>

### P203 · A｜Should children learn to use AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 适度使用提高效率，独立练习避免能力弱化；用儿童学习例子。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think children can learn to use AI as long as they still do their own thinking.
> **It can make some tasks quicker, but children need independent practice to build their skills.**
> For example, a child could try a question first, then use an AI explanation to check a mistake more quickly.
> That said, simply copying its answer won't give them much practice.
> Overall, children should learn to use AI to support their own work.

论点回查：[E2](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#2-technology--efficiency-and-over-reliance-科技提高效率--过度依赖)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P203](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p203)。

题型：Q6

**串法：** 自动化省时省力→任务更高效；软件开发例子可直接使用。

**答案：**

> AI can help us get routine tasks done more quickly.
> **It can give us a first draft to work from, so we don't have to start from scratch.**
> For example, a software engineer could use AI to draft a small piece of code, then check and improve it.
> So, it can save time on everyday work.

<a id="p205"></a>

### P205 · A｜Do you think students are overly reliant on AI?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M06 长期能力与短期便利

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 过度便利→减少主动努力→依赖与独立思考弱化；限定部分学生。

**答案：**

> I think some students are overly reliant on AI.
> **When they let a tool do most of the work, they get less practice doing it themselves.**
> For example, a student who always copies an AI answer may avoid working through the question independently.
> That said, occasional help is not the same as complete dependence.
> So, the problem starts when students let AI do the thinking for them.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

### P233 · A｜What are the advantages of watching sports events online?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB07](#ab07)

**串法：** 快捷获取赛事、省时省力→观看方便；换观看场景即可。

**答案：**

> Watching sports online is convenient because it saves people the trip to a venue.
> **They can follow a match from home and fit it around their other plans.**
> For example, someone could watch a game online after work without having to travel across town.
> That said, watching alone may be less exciting than being with other fans.
> Overall, it makes watching sport easier to fit into a busy day.

### P255 · B｜What are the differences between watching movies at home and in a cinema?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB21](#ab21)

**串法：** 在家观看省出行，影院提供专门设施 → 便利与观影环境不同 → 双侧比较。

**答案：**

> Watching at home is convenient, while a cinema gives you a big screen and a space just for watching films.
> **At home, you don't need to travel; at the cinema, the screen can make the film more enjoyable**.
> For example, someone could watch a film on their sofa after work, but visit the cinema for a bigger screen.
> So, it depends on how much the viewing experience matters to them.

<a id="p309"></a>

### P309 · B｜Are there any differences between the food people eat today and the food people ate in the past?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB13 主链 / 对照](#ab13)；辅助链：[AB19](#ab19)

**串法：** 家庭传统延续，而当前日常也可选择方便的外卖 → 获取方式改变但并非旧饮食全消失。

**答案：**

> One difference is that people now have easier access to quick meals, although traditional dishes are still part of their diets.
> **Convenience can shape what they eat on busy days, while family traditions still influence special meals.**
> For example, a family might now order takeaway during the week but still make dumplings for Lunar New Year, as they did in the past.
> So, everyday meals can change while food traditions carry on.

**学习提示：** 只比较个人日常和延续的家庭传统；不虚构全国饮食结构的历史数据。

### P310 · B｜Do people today prefer eating at home or in a restaurant?

> **Eating out means less cooking and washing up**, while cooking at home can give family or friends meaningful time together.
> For example, friends might choose a restaurant for a convenient dinner, while relatives may prefer cooking together at home during a festival.
> So, the occasion and the experience people want can change their preference.
