<a id="ab14"></a>

## AB14 核实信息并比较 → 更合适的判断

**怎么串：** 用电脑、岗位或课程选择说明核对什么、怎么比较。决定前的判断和决定后的执行计划分清。

- **主链：** reliable information from varied sources → informed judgement → better decisions
- **反向 / 对照：** misinformation or overload → distorted judgement → poor choices

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P186](#p186) | B | 了解岗位要求 → 选相关经历并练习解释 → 面试时能清楚展示匹配。 |
| [P194](#p194) | A | 核实信息、比较优先级→较好决定；日常小事可加轻量快速决策限定。 |
| [P196](#p196) | B | 核对新旧岗位的真实待遇、工作和成长条件 → 比较是否符合需要 → 换工作好坏取决于匹配。 |
| [P197](#p197) | B | 晋升可能伴随不同待遇与责任 → 核对实际条件 → 判断是否符合本人需要。 |
| [P208](#p208) | B | 正在处理事情或回复需要查证 → 暂时无法提供可靠答案 → 不立即回复。 |
| [P228](#p228) | B | 家庭与学校问题不同 → 父母和老师掌握的相关经验不同 → 按问题选意见来源。 |
| [P248](#p248) | B | 儿童也可能面临影响较久的学校选择 → 需要理解选项并求助 → 有时确实要做重要决定。 |
| [P249](#p249) | B | 毕业后继续学习、培训或工作 → 核对各自要求和本人目标 → 作下一步选择。 |
| [P253](#p253) | A | 搜集可靠信息、比较重要条件→作出选择；再安排执行，区分判断与计划。 |
| [P266](#p266) | B | 本地新闻服务身边决策，全国新闻提供更广背景 → 根据所需信息选择 → 不概括全国偏好。 |
| [P286](#p286) | A | 选择可靠、清楚的信息作为最重要因素→合理判断与信任。 |

<a id="p186"></a>

### P186 · B｜What should people do to prepare for a job interview?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 了解岗位要求 → 选相关经历并练习解释 → 面试时能清楚展示匹配。

**答案：**

> People should read the job description and prepare a few examples of relevant work they've done.
> **Knowing what the employer is looking for** helps them choose what to talk about.
> For example, someone applying for a software job could practise explaining a project, the problem they solved and their own part in it.
> So, good preparation means understanding the job and being ready to explain why you're suitable.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md) · [Describe a time when you worked in a group](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20worked%20in%20a%20group.md)

论点回查：[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P186](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p186)。

<a id="p194"></a>

### P194 · A｜Is it better to make a decision quickly or to take time to think it through?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 核实信息、比较优先级→较好决定；日常小事可加轻量快速决策限定。

**答案：**

> For an important decision, I would usually take time to think it through rather than decide immediately.
> **Taking a little longer gives people time to compare the options and decide what matters most.**
> For example, someone choosing between two jobs can compare the salary, location and growth opportunities before accepting an offer.
> So, important choices usually deserve some careful thought.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md) · [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md)

论点回查：[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P194](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p194)。

<a id="p196"></a>

### P196 · B｜Do you think it is good to change jobs?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 核对新旧岗位的真实待遇、工作和成长条件 → 比较是否符合需要 → 换工作好坏取决于匹配。

**答案：**

> Changing jobs can be a good move if the new role is a better fit.
> **Checking the pay, duties, location and chances to learn helps people decide whether the move is worthwhile.**
> For example, a developer could check whether a new role offers better learning opportunities, suitable pay and a manageable commute before deciding.
> That said, starting a new job can also bring extra pressure.
> Overall, it depends on what the person would gain and give up.

**学习提示：** 不把换工作等同失业，也不假设新岗位一定培训或加薪。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an important decision that you made](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20important%20decision%20that%20you%20made.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example)、[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living) · [审计 P196](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p196)。

<a id="p197"></a>

### P197 · B｜Is it good for people to get a job promotion?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 晋升可能伴随不同待遇与责任 → 核对实际条件 → 判断是否符合本人需要。

**答案：**

> A promotion can be a good thing if it brings better pay and work the person wants to do.
> **They need to weigh those benefits against the extra responsibility.**
> For example, someone might welcome a pay rise but hesitate if managing a team would mean much longer hours.
> So, a promotion is worthwhile when the benefits fit the person's needs.

**学习提示：** 具体晋升待遇未在素材中提供，采用评价标准，不声称所有晋升加薪。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a well-organized person you know](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20well-organized%20person%20you%20know.md) · [Describe a long-term goal or ambition you would like to achieve](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20long-term%20goal%20or%20ambition%20you%20would%20like%20to%20achieve.md)

论点回查：[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living)、[E50](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康)、[E51](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5051-constant-pressure-and-well-being-持续压力与身心健康) · [审计 P197](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p197)。

<a id="p208"></a>

### P208 · B｜In what situations do people not respond to messages right away?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 正在处理事情或回复需要查证 → 暂时无法提供可靠答案 → 不立即回复。

**答案：**

> People may not reply straight away because they're busy or need to check something first.
> **They might not have the information they need to give a useful answer yet.**
> For example, a colleague who's asked about a technical problem may need to look through the project before explaining what's wrong.
> So, a slow reply can mean they're busy finding an accurate answer.

**学习提示：** 技术查证为从项目消息素材迁移的假设；不改写 Stella 实际迟回的原因。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you sent a message or an email to someone but received no reply for a long time](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20sent%20a%20message%20or%20an%20email%20to%20someone%20but%20received%20no%20reply%20for%20a%20long%20time.md)

论点回查：[E26](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#26-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-1)、[E31](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#31-effects-of-unclear-communication-沟通不清的影响) · [审计 P208](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p208)。

<a id="p228"></a>

### P228 · B｜Whose opinions are more important to children, their parents' or teachers'?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 家庭与学校问题不同 → 父母和老师掌握的相关经验不同 → 按问题选意见来源。

**答案：**

> It depends on what the child needs advice about.
> **Teachers may know more about school choices, while parents know the child's life at home better**.
> For example, a teacher could explain which course matches a child's strengths, while a parent helps them think about the time it would take.
> So, children may value different opinions for different decisions.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

<a id="p248"></a>

### P248 · B｜Do you think children sometimes have to make important decisions?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 儿童也可能面临影响较久的学校选择 → 需要理解选项并求助 → 有时确实要做重要决定。

**答案：**

> Yes, children sometimes have to make choices that affect their learning for quite a while.
> **Advice can help them understand the options and choose carefully.**
> For example, a child choosing a term-long school activity could ask a parent or teacher about the commitment before signing up.
> That said, adults should help with choices beyond the child's experience.
> Overall, children do face important decisions, even if they need support with them.

**串法：** 毕业后继续学习、培训或工作 → 核对各自要求和本人目标 → 作下一步选择。

**答案：**

> Teenagers may need to decide whether to continue studying, take a training course or look for work.
> **Checking what each option involves helps them choose something that fits their goals.**
> For example, a school leaver interested in technology could compare the skills taught on a course with those they'd learn in a training placement.
> So, a major decision is what direction to take after school.

<a id="p253"></a>

### P253 · A｜How do people usually make important decision?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)；辅助链：[AB03](#ab03)

**串法：** 搜集可靠信息、比较重要条件→作出选择；再安排执行，区分判断与计划。

**答案：**

> People usually gather information and compare their options before making an important decision.
> **Checking the facts and knowing what matters most help them choose something that fits their needs.**
> For example, someone comparing two job offers could confirm the pay, location and duties, then decide which suits their priorities.
> So, people often make these choices by checking the details and weighing things up.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E27](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#27-what-helps-people-make-an-informed-decision--version-2--example) · [审计 P253](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p253)。

<a id="p266"></a>

### P266 · B｜Do people in your country prefer following local news or national news?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T09 年龄与代际

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 本地新闻服务身边决策，全国新闻提供更广背景 → 根据所需信息选择 → 不概括全国偏好。

**答案：**

> I don't think everyone in China prefers the same type of news.
> **Local news helps people make decisions about nearby places, while national news gives them a broader picture.**
> For example, someone might check a local report about beach conditions before a visit, then read national news to understand a wider issue.
> So, I think people choose according to what they want to know.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P266](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p266)。

<a id="p286"></a>

### P286 · A｜What is the most important factor in an advertisement?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB14 主链 / 对照](#ab14)

**串法：** 选择可靠、清楚的信息作为最重要因素→合理判断与信任。

**答案：**

> I think the most important thing in an advert is clear, reliable information.
> **People need to understand what's being offered before they can tell whether it suits them.**
> For example, an advert for a course should explain what students will learn instead of promising unrealistic results.
> That said, it's still sensible to check other sources.
> Overall, a useful advert gives people an honest picture of the product.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time when you received good service from a staff member in a shop](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20time%20when%20you%20received%20good%20service%20from%20a%20staff%20member%20in%20a%20shop.md) · [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements) · [审计 P286](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p286)。

<a id="ab15"></a>

## AB15 曝光与榜样 → 关注、模仿和机会

**怎么串：** 本节取曝光与说服分支：谁被看见、谁受影响、产生什么选择。隐私压力单列 AB16，不因同属 T06 就混成一节。

- **主链：** visibility and credibility → greater public attention → wider influence or opportunity
- **反向 / 对照：** persuasive advertising or constant public exposure → less careful judgement or loss of privacy → misleading choices or stress

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P007](#p007) | B | 过去主要接触身边受众 → 现在作品可以面向更大范围展示 → 更容易被注意，但不保证持久受欢迎。 |
| [P068](#p068) | B | 看到医生帮助他人 → 把医生当榜样 → 向往相同职业。 |
| [P154](#p154) | A | 有说服力的广告诱导购买非必需品 → 浪费钱和资源 → 鼓励谨慎消费。 |
| [P181](#p181) | B | 运动员等公众人物和相关专家 → 受众关注其经历或信息 → 获得采访机会。 |
| [P184](#p184) | B | 公众关注作品、赛事或经历 → 名人谈准备、困难和选择 → 满足受众对具体经历的关注。 |
| [P251](#p251) | A | 说服与名人示范→购买超出需要→影响选择。 |
| [P252](#p252) | A | 曝光带来机会；夸大诱导→差选择，核实信息作限定。 |
| [P284](#p284) | A | 曝光带来商业机会，夸大或盲从导致差选择；已有完整利弊。 |
| [P285](#p285) | A | 名人关注与粉丝模仿→购买影响；可用已练代言例子说明有效但有限。 |
| [P288](#p288) | A | 增加产品曝光→注意力与销售机会→企业收入。 |
| [P289](#p289) | B | 线上可接触远方目标受众，线下可触达当地人 → 看受众在哪里 → 曝光方式须匹配。 |

<a id="p007"></a>

### P007 · B｜Is it easier to become popular nowadays?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q3

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 过去主要接触身边受众 → 现在作品可以面向更大范围展示 → 更容易被注意，但不保证持久受欢迎。

**答案：**

> In some ways, yes, because it's easier to reach people now.
> **Sharing work online lets someone get noticed well beyond their local area.**
> For example, a young singer can post a performance for listeners around the world instead of relying on a small local audience.
> That said, a brief burst of attention doesn't guarantee lasting popularity.
> So, getting noticed may be easier, but staying popular is another matter.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a popular person](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20popular%20person.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising) · [审计 P007](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p007)。

<a id="p068"></a>

### P068 · B｜Why do some children want to become doctors?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 看到医生帮助他人 → 把医生当榜样 → 向往相同职业。

**答案：**

> Some children want to become doctors because they've seen a doctor help someone and admire what they do.
> **That person can become a role model and influence the child's ambitions.**
> For example, a child who sees a doctor caring for a relative might want to help people in the same way.
> So, a good experience with a doctor can spark an interest in the job.

**学习提示：** 使用 M05 榜样模仿分支；不把“医疗重要”当作儿童择业的直接原因。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person you know who would like to choose a career in the medical field (e.g. a doctor, a nurse)](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20would%20like%20to%20choose%20a%20career%20in%20the%20medical%20field%20%28e.g.%20a%20doctor%2C%20a%20nurse%29.md)

论点回查：[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption)、[E57](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#57-how-can-governments-support-peoples-healthwell-being) · [审计 P068](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p068)。

<a id="p154"></a>

### P154 · A｜Should parents encourage their children to spend money freely or to be careful with it?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物 ＋ T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)；辅助链：[AB17](#ab17)

**串法：** 有说服力的广告诱导购买非必需品 → 浪费钱和资源 → 鼓励谨慎消费。

**答案：**

> I think parents should encourage children to be careful with money.
> **Adverts can make things look more appealing than they really are, so children may waste money on things they don't need.**
> For example, a child might spend all their pocket money on an advertised toy and then barely play with it.
> That said, being careful doesn't mean never buying something fun.
> So, children should learn to think before they spend.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption)、[E55](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#55-how-can-losing-a-job-affect-peoples-standard-of-living) · [审计 P154](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p154)。

<a id="p181"></a>

### P181 · B｜What kinds of people are frequently interviewed?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 运动员等公众人物和相关专家 → 受众关注其经历或信息 → 获得采访机会。

**答案：**

> Famous people and experts are often interviewed.
> **People want to hear from them because their work or experience is interesting or useful.**
> For example, a badminton player might be interviewed after a big match to explain how they approached it.
> So, people are often interviewed for experiences or knowledge that interest an audience.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E56](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#56-what-are-the-disadvantages-of-being-a-celebrity) · [审计 P181](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p181)。

<a id="p184"></a>

### P184 · B｜What kinds of things do famous people often say in an interview?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 公众关注作品、赛事或经历 → 名人谈准备、困难和选择 → 满足受众对具体经历的关注。

**答案：**

> Famous people may talk about their work, recent achievements and the difficulties they faced.
> **Talking about what they went through** gives the audience more than a list of results.
> For example, a badminton player might describe changing tactics after losing the first game of a final.
> So, interviews often focus on what happened and how the person responded.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E56](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#56-what-are-the-disadvantages-of-being-a-celebrity) · [审计 P184](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p184)。

<a id="p251"></a>

### P251 · A｜Do you think advertisements can influence our decisions when shopping?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 说服与名人示范→购买超出需要→影响选择。

**答案：**

> Yes, adverts can influence what we buy.
> **A convincing message or a celebrity recommendation can make a product seem more appealing.**
> For example, a fan might buy shoes promoted by their favourite athlete even though they don't need another pair.
> That said, checking independent information can help people make a more careful choice.
> Overall, adverts can influence our decisions, even if we don't always act on them.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P251](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p251)。

<a id="p252"></a>

### P252 · A｜Do you think the influence of advertising is good?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 曝光带来机会；夸大诱导→差选择，核实信息作限定。

**答案：**

> I think advertising can be helpful, but its influence isn't always positive.
> **It helps people discover products, yet it can also make them buy without thinking carefully**.
> For example, an advert might introduce a useful course but exaggerate how quickly students will improve.
> So, advertising helps most when people check the claims before spending money.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P252](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p252)。

<a id="p284"></a>

### P284 · A｜What are the advantages and disadvantages of advertisements?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 曝光带来商业机会，夸大或盲从导致差选择；已有完整利弊。

**答案：**

> Adverts can help businesses get noticed, but they can also mislead customers.
> **More people hearing about a product or service means more chances to sell it.**
> For example, an advert for a local repair service could bring in customers who hadn't heard of it before.
> However, exaggerated claims can persuade people to buy things they don't really need.
> Overall, adverts can be useful, but their claims need checking.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E15](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#15-why-should-consumers-fact-check-advertisements)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P284](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p284)。

<a id="p285"></a>

### P285 · A｜Why are many advertisements endorsed by celebrities? How useful are they?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 名人关注与粉丝模仿→购买影响；可用已练代言例子说明有效但有限。

**答案：**

> Companies use celebrities because they attract attention and can influence their fans.
> **Seeing someone they admire use a product** may make fans want to try it too.
> For example, a fan might buy a drink after seeing their favourite athlete in its advert.
> That said, liking the celebrity doesn't mean they'll like the product.
> So, celebrity adverts can encourage sales, but they don't work on everyone.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P285](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p285)。

<a id="p288"></a>

### P288 · A｜Is advertising important for a company? Why?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)；辅助链：[AB12](#ab12)

**串法：** 增加产品曝光→注意力与销售机会→企业收入。

**答案：**

> Yes, advertising can be important because it helps a company get noticed.
> **When more people know about a business, it has a better chance of attracting customers.**
> For example, a new cleaning service can advertise locally so residents know that the service is available.
> That said, advertising cannot replace a product or service people actually need.
> So, advertising helps a business reach people who might need what it sells.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P288](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p288)。

<a id="p289"></a>

### P289 · B｜Which is more effective, online advertising or offline advertising?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M05 社会影响与模仿

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB15 主链 / 对照](#ab15)

**串法：** 线上可接触远方目标受众，线下可触达当地人 → 看受众在哪里 → 曝光方式须匹配。

**答案：**

> I don't think either is always more effective; it depends on who the business wants to reach.
> **Online adverts can reach people in many places, while offline adverts can work well for nearby customers.**
> For example, a sports-shoe brand could reach fans through an online video, while a local shop could put up a poster where residents pass every day.
> So, the best choice is the one the right customers are likely to see.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an advertisement with a famous person in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20advertisement%20with%20a%20famous%20person%20in%20it.md) · [Describe a person you know who has a successful business](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20you%20know%20who%20has%20a%20successful%20business.md)

论点回查：[E3](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3-technology--internet-reshaping-habits-互联网重塑习惯)、[E14](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#14-what-are-the-advantages-and-disadvantages-of-advertising) · [审计 P289](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p289)。

<a id="ab16"></a>

## AB16 过度公开私人信息 → 暴露感与压力

**怎么串：** 本节只取 T06 的公开曝光—隐私—压力分支。拍照和采访都要说明什么私人情境被暴露。

- **主链：** persuasive advertising or constant public exposure → less careful judgement or loss of privacy → misleading choices or stress

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P023](#p023) | B | 私人场景被拍摄 → 当事人感到暴露 → 不应把所有场所都视作适合拍照。 |
| [P182](#p182) | A | 采访追问私人生活 → 感到暴露和压力 → 不愿被采访。 |

<a id="p023"></a>

### P023 · B｜Do you think it's ok to take photos everywhere?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB16 主链 / 对照](#ab16)

**串法：** 私人场景被拍摄 → 当事人感到暴露 → 不应把所有场所都视作适合拍照。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think it's appropriate to take photos everywhere.
> **Photographing people in private moments can make them feel exposed and uncomfortable.**
> For example, a stranger resting in a hospital might feel uncomfortable if someone took a close-up photo of them.
> That said, asking permission can make a difference in some situations.
> Overall, people should consider the setting and how others might feel.

**学习提示：** 讨论隐私和礼貌，不把例子说成具体法律禁令。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E52](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#52-how-can-technology-affect-peoples-privacytrust)、[E53](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#53-technologyprivacy--developed-version)、[E56](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#56-what-are-the-disadvantages-of-being-a-celebrity) · [审计 P023](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p023)。

<a id="p182"></a>

### P182 · A｜Why do some people dislike being interviewed?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T06 科技、互联网、信息与影响

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB16 主链 / 对照](#ab16)；辅助链：[AB15](#ab15)

**串法：** 采访追问私人生活 → 感到暴露和压力 → 不愿被采访。

**答案：**

> Some people dislike interviews because they feel uncomfortable with constant public attention.
> **Questions about their private lives can make them feel exposed and stressed.**
> For example, a celebrity may feel overwhelmed when an interviewer keeps asking about private family matters.
> So, concerns about privacy can make people reluctant to be interviewed.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a time you watched a famous person being interviewed](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20a%20time%20you%20watched%20a%20famous%20person%20being%20interviewed.md)

论点回查：[E56](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#56-what-are-the-disadvantages-of-being-a-celebrity) · [审计 P182](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p182)。

<a id="ab17"></a>

## AB17 便利绿色选择、减少浪费 → 环境改善

**怎么串：** 替代用品或设施怎样改变行为必须明确。已有素材中的海岸塑料禁令是提议，不能据此回答现行法。

- **主链：** practical infrastructure and incentives → encourage greener choices → lower pollution and resource waste
- **反向 / 对照：** excessive consumption → waste and pollution → long-term environmental damage

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P033](#p033) | A | 企业减少一次性用品或提供绿色选择→少浪费与污染。 |
| [P110](#p110) | B | 骑行设施更方便 → 骑车需要的时间精力降低 → 使用可能增加；设施差则限制预测。 |
| [P134](#p134) | B | 游客活动产生垃圾 → 清理压力和环境干扰增加 → 居民承担代价。 |
| [P306](#p306) | B | Part 2 只有希望颁布的海岸一次性塑料禁令；还缺至少一部本人知道的现行环保法律或规则及其实际要求。 |

<a id="p033"></a>

### P033 · A｜How can companies help protect the environment?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q6

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** 企业减少一次性用品或提供绿色选择→少浪费与污染。

**答案：**

> Companies can help by reducing waste and making greener choices easier for customers.
> **Using fewer disposable items can cut the amount of rubbish they produce.**
> For example, a shop can encourage customers to bring reusable bags instead of handing out single-use plastic bags.
> So, businesses can protect the environment through small but consistent changes.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an interesting video](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md) · [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E1](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#1-environmental-protection-环境保护)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P033](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p033)。

<a id="p110"></a>

### P110 · B｜Do you think people will use bicycles more or less frequently in cities in the future?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q4

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** 骑行设施更方便 → 骑车需要的时间精力降低 → 使用可能增加；设施差则限制预测。

**答案：**

> I think people may cycle more in cities that make it easier to get around by bike.
> **Well-connected cycle lanes and convenient bike parking can save time and effort on everyday journeys.**
> For example, someone with a slow drive to work might switch to cycling if there's a direct route and somewhere to park their bike.
> That said, awkward routes could put people off.
> So, it partly depends on whether cities make cycling a practical choice.

**学习提示：** 借用堵车通勤场景；车道改善是条件预测，不假装已经建设。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a short trip you often take but do not enjoy](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20short%20trip%20you%20often%20take%20but%20do%20not%20enjoy.md)

论点回查：[E1](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#1-environmental-protection-环境保护) · [审计 P110](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p110)。

<a id="p134"></a>

### P134 · B｜What are the disadvantages for people living in popular tourist cities?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物 ＋ T04 公平、规则与公共资源

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** 游客活动产生垃圾 → 清理压力和环境干扰增加 → 居民承担代价。

**答案：**

> One problem is the extra rubbish left in busy tourist areas.
> **Residents may have to deal with dirtier surroundings and more cleaning**.
> For example, after a busy holiday, people living near a beach might find plastic waste where they usually walk.
> So, poorly managed tourist waste can make residents' surroundings less pleasant.

**学习提示：** 引用现有地方新闻练习素材，不当作本轮核实的新闻报道。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a piece of local news that people are interested in](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20piece%20of%20local%20news%20that%20people%20are%20interested%20in.md)

论点回查：[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption)、[E21](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#21-how-should-governments-spend-public-money) · [审计 P134](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p134)。

<a id="p306"></a>

### P306 · B｜What environmental laws does your country already have?

**状态：待补关键事实；暂不生成英文答案。**

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M02 时间与精力成本

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB17 主链 / 对照](#ab17)

**串法：** Part 2 只有希望颁布的海岸一次性塑料禁令；还缺至少一部本人知道的现行环保法律或规则及其实际要求。

**学习提示：** 当前素材不能自然覆盖原题所需事实；保留题目与缺口，不把假设写成现行事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe an environmental law you would like your country to introduce](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20environmental%20law%20you%20would%20like%20your%20country%20to%20introduce.md)

论点回查：[E1](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#1-environmental-protection-环境保护)、[E54](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#54-how-can-advertising-lead-to-excessive-consumption) · [审计 P306](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p306)。

<a id="ab18"></a>

## AB18 照料与陪伴 → 情感支持和责任

**怎么串：** 宠物优势和照料负担放在同一节。Part 2 仅有动物故事与自然素材，具体养宠场景采用假设，不冒充已有养宠经历。

- **主链：** daily care and companionship → empathy and emotional support / build a strong bond with → stronger responsibility
- **反向 / 对照：** cost and long-term care needs → pressure on owners → possible neglect

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P278](#p278) | B | 认识动物的日常需要 → 理解照料责任 → 可培养同理心，不要求每人养宠物。 |
| [P279](#p279) | A | 陪伴责任的好处与长期照料成本两面，城市场景加适当条件。 |
| [P280](#p280) | A | 日常照料与陪伴→情感纽带→视作家人。 |
| [P281](#p281) | B | 素材缺养宠数量观察 → 只作有限判断并解释陪伴价值 → 不声称有数量证据。 |
| [P282](#p282) | A | 陪伴→情感支持；日常照料→责任感。 |

<a id="p278"></a>

### P278 · B｜Should schools teach children about animals?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 认识动物的日常需要 → 理解照料责任 → 可培养同理心，不要求每人养宠物。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think schools should teach children about animals and how to look after them.
> **Understanding an animal's daily needs helps children see that caring for it is a responsibility.**
> For example, a class could work out who would feed a pet, give it water and care for it during the school holidays.
> They don't need to own a pet to learn these things.
> Overall, these lessons can help children treat animals with care.

**学习提示：** 从动物与自然素材作教育场景迁移，不编写学生实际养宠经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility) · [审计 P278](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p278)。

<a id="p279"></a>

### P279 · A｜Some people think pets should not be kept in cities. What do you think?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 陪伴责任的好处与长期照料成本两面，城市场景加适当条件。

**答案：**

> I don't think living in a city should automatically stop someone from keeping a pet.
> **Caring for an animal can give people comfort and a sense of responsibility.**
> For example, someone living in a city flat could build a close bond with a cat by feeding it and spending time with it every day.
> However, the cost and long-term care can put pressure on owners.
> Overall, what matters is whether the owner can meet the animal's needs.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility)、[E13](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#13-pets--drawback) · [审计 P279](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p279)。

<a id="p280"></a>

### P280 · A｜Many people regard pets as members of their family. What do you think?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 日常照料与陪伴→情感纽带→视作家人。

**答案：**

> I can understand why many people see pets as family members.
> **Daily care and companionship help owners build a strong bond with their animals.**
> For example, someone who feeds a dog and spends time with it every day may rely on that companionship for comfort.
> That said, people differ in how close they feel to animals.
> Overall, a lasting bond can make a pet feel like part of the family.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility) · [审计 P280](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p280)。

<a id="p281"></a>

### P281 · B｜Do many people keep pets in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M03 自主权与责任感

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 素材缺养宠数量观察 → 只作有限判断并解释陪伴价值 → 不声称有数量证据。

**答案：**

> My impression is that quite a few people in China have pets, though I don't know how common it is overall.
> **An animal can give people company and comfort.**
> For example, someone who cares for a cat every day might build such a close bond that they see it as part of the family.
> So, I think pet ownership is fairly common, but I couldn't give a reliable figure.

**学习提示：** Part 2 无养宠统计或个人养宠记录；本题为有限判断与假设例子，不作为已核实事实。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E13](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#13-pets--drawback) · [审计 P281](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p281)。

<a id="p282"></a>

### P282 · A｜What are the advantages of keeping a pet?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T07 环境与动物

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB18 主链 / 对照](#ab18)

**串法：** 陪伴→情感支持；日常照料→责任感。

**答案：**

> Keeping a pet gives people company and helps them learn to be responsible.
> **Meeting an animal's daily needs can build a close bond and a habit of caring for it.**
> For example, a cat owner needs to feed their pet every day and may find spending time with it comforting.
> That said, the animal still needs care when its owner is busy.
> Overall, pets can offer companionship while teaching people to look after another living thing.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a story or book with animals in it](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20story%20or%20book%20with%20animals%20in%20it.md) · [Describe an interesting video](../part2/❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20an%20interesting%20video.md)

论点回查：[E11](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#11-pets--emotional-support)、[E12](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#12-pets--responsibility) · [审计 P282](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p282)。

<a id="ab19"></a>

## AB19 参与文化与了解过去 → 理解和传承

**怎么串：** 旧建筑、历史、旅行与节日食物共用文化参与链。个人家里的习惯不自动代表家乡或全国。

- **主链：** participation and cultural exposure → stronger identity and understanding → pass down traditions in a living way
- **反向 / 对照：** commercialisation poses a threat to our traditional customs/  or dominant global content → weaker local engagement → cultural loss

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P041](#p041) | A | 手工艺呈现传统→文化理解；有用产品销售→收入与就业。 |
| [P049](#p049) | B | Part 2 只写叔叔制作木制家具，未确认它是湛江传统工艺；还缺一种本人了解的家乡传统工艺及其本地联系。 |
| [P051](#p051) | A | 早期文化接触→了解传统与身份；举适合儿童的故事即可，不断言最佳年龄。 |
| [P052](#p052) | B | 能听懂简单故事时接触历史 → 认识过去生活 → 建立文化理解；内容按理解能力调整。 |
| [P053](#p053) | B | 家人讲故事、看旧照片或参观 → 接触具体过去生活 → 学前儿童形成初步历史理解。 |
| [P054](#p054) | A | 了解过去的生活→理解历史传统→身份与传承。 |
| [P056](#p056) | B | 普通生活、衣食和习俗 → 看到历史如何影响日常 → 更完整理解文化。 |
| [P099](#p099) | A | 历史建筑→文化记忆与身份→保存传统。 |
| [P100](#p100) | A | 文化价值与实际功能→决定保留或改造；不把所有旧建筑一概而论。 |
| [P126](#p126) | B | 列城市文化游与海边休闲两类 → 不同目的对应不同活动 → 用现有北京、海边素材。 |
| [P144](#p144) | B | 春节等特殊日子 → 家庭共同准备食物和相聚 → 传统在参与中延续。 |
| [P145](#p145) | B | 本人和 Stella 的海外旅行 → 接触不同文化和生活 → 部分人喜欢；不推全国比例。 |
| [P148](#p148) | B | 旅行亲身参与，书籍提供文字背景 → 理解方式不同 → 按想了解的内容选择。 |
| [P257](#p257) | A | 电影呈现当地生活与传统 → 观众获得文化背景 → 更好理解，但一部电影不能代表全国。 |
| [P269](#p269) | A | 理解共同历史传统→身份归属→文化延续。 |
| [P270](#p270) | A | 参与文化活动、了解遗产→增强身份理解。 |
| [P307](#p307) | A | 共同食物习俗→文化身份与家庭联系→传统延续。 |
| [P308](#p308) | B | 日常食物侧重一餐需要，节日食物有共同准备与传统意义 → 活动和意义不同。 |
| [P311](#p311) | B | 家庭中的饺子等熟悉食物 → 与共同习俗相关 → 举具体例子不概括全国最流行。 |

<a id="p041"></a>

### P041 · A｜Are traditional handicrafts important to tourism?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T05 经济与就业

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB12](#ab12)

**串法：** 手工艺呈现传统→文化理解；有用产品销售→收入与就业。

**答案：**

> Yes, traditional handicrafts can be an important part of tourism.
> **Visitors can learn about local traditions, while craftspeople earn money from selling their work.**
> For example, a visitor could watch a local craftsperson demonstrate a traditional way of making bowls and then buy one to take home.
> That said, not every visitor is interested in handicrafts.
> Overall, they can help visitors understand a place and support the people who make them.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md) · [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E16](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#16-how-can-successful-companies-contribute-to-society--the-local-economy) · [审计 P041](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p041)。

**学习提示：** 当前素材不能自然覆盖原题所需事实；保留题目与缺口，不把假设写成现行事实。

<a id="p051"></a>

### P051 · A｜Do you think children should begin learning history from an early age?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 早期文化接触→了解传统与身份；举适合儿童的故事即可，不断言最佳年龄。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think children can start learning about history through simple stories from an early age.
> **Hearing about how people lived helps them understand where familiar traditions come from.**
> For example, a parent could take a child to an old local building and tell them a short story about a custom associated with it.
> That said, the story needs to be simple enough for the child to follow.
> Overall, it's a good way to start helping children understand the past.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md) · [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P051](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p051)。

<a id="p052"></a>

### P052 · B｜At what age do you think people should begin to learn history?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 能听懂简单故事时接触历史 → 认识过去生活 → 建立文化理解；内容按理解能力调整。

**答案：**

> I think children can start before primary school, once they're able to follow a simple story.
> **Stories about family life and familiar traditions make the past easier to understand.**
> For example, a parent could use old photos to explain how the child's grandparents celebrated a festival.
> That said, there's no need to begin with complicated dates or political events.
> Overall, the starting point should suit what the child can understand.

**学习提示：** 这是教育建议，不把某一年龄说成科学统一门槛。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md) · [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P052](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p052)。

<a id="p053"></a>

### P053 · B｜How do children learn history before they attend school?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 家人讲故事、看旧照片或参观 → 接触具体过去生活 → 学前儿童形成初步历史理解。

**答案：**

> Before school, children can learn history through family stories, old photographs and simple visits to heritage sites.
> **Connecting the past with people or places they recognise** can make it easier to understand.
> For example, a grandparent might use an old family photo to describe what home life was like years ago.
> So, everyday family activities can provide a first introduction to history.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md) · [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P053](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p053)。

<a id="p054"></a>

### P054 · A｜Is it important to learn how people in the past lived their lives?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 了解过去的生活→理解历史传统→身份与传承。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think learning about ordinary life in the past is important.
> **It helps people understand their history and the traditions that have been passed down.**
> For example, learning how families celebrated a festival can explain why certain customs still matter today.
> That said, we do not need to copy every old custom.
> Overall, understanding past lives can give us a clearer sense of our culture.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E10](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#10-heritage-sites--simplified-version) · [审计 P054](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p054)。

<a id="p056"></a>

### P056 · B｜Besides big historical events, what else is important when learning history?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 普通生活、衣食和习俗 → 看到历史如何影响日常 → 更完整理解文化。

**答案：**

> Ordinary people’s lives and customs are important alongside major historical events.
> They help us **understand how the past shaped everyday life**, rather than just remembering dates and leaders.
> For example, learning how families prepared food can show where food traditions today come from.
> So, history lessons should include everyday life as well as big events.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone you know who enjoys learning about history](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20you%20know%20who%20enjoys%20learning%20about%20history.md)

<a id="p099"></a>

### P099 · A｜Do you think old buildings are valuable?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 历史建筑→文化记忆与身份→保存传统。

**答案：**

> Yes, some old buildings are valuable because they connect us with local history.
> **They show us how people used to live and help us understand our own culture.**
> For example, an old family home that's open to visitors could show how families arranged their rooms and celebrated special occasions.
> That said, being old doesn't automatically make a building valuable.
> Overall, buildings that tell us something meaningful about the past are worth protecting.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p100"></a>

### P100 · A｜Do you think all old buildings should be demolished?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T10 地点、建筑与设施

机制：M01 实用价值

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB21](#ab21)

**串法：** 文化价值与实际功能→决定保留或改造；不把所有旧建筑一概而论。

**答案：**

> No, I don't think all old buildings should be demolished.
> **Some preserve cultural traditions, while others still provide useful spaces for daily life.**
> For example, an old public library can remain a place for reading while reminding residents of their town's history.
> That said, some buildings may no longer be suitable for use.
> Overall, we should consider each building's history and usefulness before demolishing it.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

<a id="p126"></a>

### P126 · B｜Where do people in your country often go for holidays?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)；辅助链：[AB05](#ab05)

**串法：** 列城市文化游与海边休闲两类 → 不同目的对应不同活动 → 用现有北京、海边素材。

**答案：**

> In China, people may go to cities, the coast or places known for their history.
> **The choice often depends on what they want to do on holiday.**
> For example, someone interested in culture might explore Beijing's old streets, while someone who wants to relax outdoors could choose a seaside town.
> So, different types of destinations appeal to people looking for different experiences.

**学习提示：** 仅列类型与示意场景，不作全国出游量排名。

<a id="p144"></a>

### P144 · B｜What special events are celebrated in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 春节等特殊日子 → 家庭共同准备食物和相聚 → 传统在参与中延续。

**答案：**

> Spring Festival is one major celebration in China.
> **Preparing food and getting together help families pass on familiar customs.**
> For example, relatives could make dumplings together for Lunar New Year, with children learning from older family members.
> So, Spring Festival brings families together and keeps traditions going.

**学习提示：** 沿用家庭饺子素材，不声称湛江每家都这样过节。

<a id="p145"></a>

### P145 · B｜Do Chinese people like travelling abroad?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 本人和 Stella 的海外旅行 → 接触不同文化和生活 → 部分人喜欢；不推全国比例。

**答案：**

> Some Chinese people enjoy travelling abroad, and I’m one of them.
> **Experiencing a different place directly** can make its food and daily life more interesting to explore.
> For example, travellers visiting another country could talk with a market vendor about unfamiliar ingredients and local dishes.
> That said, some people prefer travelling within China.
> Overall, foreign travel appeals to people who enjoy those experiences.

<a id="p148"></a>

### P148 · B｜Which is a better way to learn about a country: travelling there or reading books about it?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 旅行亲身参与，书籍提供文字背景 → 理解方式不同 → 按想了解的内容选择。

**答案：**

> I think travelling is better for experiencing daily life, while books are better for learning the background in detail.
> **Being there lets people take part, but reading can explain things they might miss on a short visit.**
> For example, someone could learn about local food by talking to a market seller, then read a book to understand the traditions behind the dishes.
> So, the better choice depends on what they want to learn.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

- [ ] 不看稿口述

**串法：** 电影呈现当地生活与传统 → 观众获得文化背景 → 更好理解，但一部电影不能代表全国。

**答案：**

> Yes, films can give people a better idea of a country's culture.
> **Stories about everyday life can introduce customs and show how people experience them.**
> For example, a film about a family festival could show how relatives prepare food and celebrate together.
> That said, one film can't represent a whole country.
> Overall, films can be a useful introduction to another culture.

### P269 · A｜Do you think it is important to have a national identity?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q5

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 理解共同历史传统→身份归属→文化延续。

**答案：**

> Yes, I think a sense of national identity can be important.
> **Knowing our shared history and traditions can help us feel that we belong and want to keep those traditions going.**
> For example, learning about a festival and celebrating it with family can connect someone with customs passed down through generations.
> That said, valuing our own culture doesn't mean rejecting other cultures.
> Overall, national identity can give people a sense of connection to their culture.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**串法：** 参与文化活动、了解遗产→增强身份理解。

**答案：**

> People can develop their national identity by learning about history and taking part in cultural traditions.
> **Joining in helps them understand what those traditions mean and feel connected to them.**
> For example, a family could visit a heritage site during a traditional festival, learn about its customs and take part in the activities.
> So, learning about culture and experiencing it can help people feel they belong.

**串法：** 共同食物习俗→文化身份与家庭联系→传统延续。

**答案：**

> People often eat special food at festivals because it's part of their family traditions.
> **Preparing and sharing familiar dishes bring relatives together and help pass those customs on.**
> For example, a family could make dumplings for Lunar New Year while older relatives show the children how it's done.
> So, special food can help families stay close and keep traditions going.

<a id="p308"></a>

### P308 · B｜What are the differences between everyday food and festival food?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统 ＋ T03 沟通、关系与合作

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 日常食物侧重一餐需要，节日食物有共同准备与传统意义 → 活动和意义不同。

**答案：**

> Everyday food is often chosen for convenience, while festival food is more closely tied to tradition.
> **Making a special dish together can be part of the celebration itself**.
> For example, a family might have a quick dinner on a weekday but spend time making dumplings together for Lunar New Year.
> So, festival food often means more than just having a meal.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage)、[E34](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#34-why-real-interaction-builds-relationships-真实互动如何建立关系) · [审计 P308](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p308)。

<a id="p311"></a>

### P311 · B｜What kinds of food are popular in your country?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T08 艺术、文化与传统

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB19 主链 / 对照](#ab19)

**串法：** 家庭中的饺子等熟悉食物 → 与共同习俗相关 → 举具体例子不概括全国最流行。

**答案：**

> Dumplings are one popular food that comes to mind, especially around Lunar New Year.
> **They can be part of a family tradition**, as well as something people enjoy eating.
> For example, relatives might make dumplings together and chat while they prepare them.
> So, dumplings are one example, though favourite foods vary across China.

**学习提示：** 例子取自下列 Part 2 素材或其明确标示的情境迁移；不据此推断全国比例。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a kind of food that you ate at special occasions](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20kind%20of%20food%20that%20you%20ate%20at%20special%20occasions.md)

论点回查：[E9](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#9-historical-sites-and-cultural-heritage) · [审计 P311](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p311)。

<a id="ab20"></a>

## AB20 经验、熟悉度与责任不同 → 需要和习惯不同

**怎么串：** 比较两侧的经验或日程，补具体活动；不把年龄当作能力、兴趣或偏好的唯一原因。第二条是可能的变化方向，不是必然预测。

- **主链：** different experience, different levels of digital familiarity and responsibilities → different needs and habits
- **反向 / 对照：** easier technology and wider education →  generational gap might gradually narrow

| 原题 | 等级 | 这题必须补出的桥梁 |
|---|---|---|
| [P001](#p001) | B | 年长者可能更有健康问题的切身体会 → 更重视日常习惯；年轻人也可能因运动目标关注健康。 |
| [P057](#p057) | B | 年轻人与长者经验不同 → 可分别分享数字操作和生活经验 → 双向学习。 |
| [P059](#p059) | A | 经历、数字熟悉度与责任不同→需求习惯不同；再补变化限定。 |
| [P060](#p060) | B | 是否工作、照顾家庭及个人习惯不同 → 日常活动不同 → 列举时不把老人都当退休者。 |
| [P078](#p078) | B | 部分儿童学习时间较固定，成人受工作家庭占用 → 练习机会不同 → 有时儿童学起来较容易。 |
| [P127](#p127) | B | 年轻人的兴趣与可用假期不同 → 城市、海边等选择不同 → 用本人及 Stella 的旅行说明。 |
| [P146](#p146) | B | 旅行兴趣、经验和责任不按年龄固定分布 → 青年与长者都可能喜欢 → 不能用年龄单独排名。 |
| [P163](#p163) | B | 儿童可能有固定练习时段，成人可能有工作家庭责任 → 练习机会不一 → 不按年龄保证速度。 |
| [P164](#p164) | B | 儿童多从基础任务开始，成人常围绕工作或生活需要 → 学习目标和安排不同。 |
| [P179](#p179) | B | 工作、退休与家庭安排不同 → 晚间可用时间和活动不同 → 给两侧但保留重叠。 |
| [P193](#p193) | B | 二十多岁可能涉及工作等选择，后续年龄也有职业家庭变化 → 不能推重要决定多数集中该阶段。 |
| [P199](#p199) | B | 年轻人可能经历学习到工作的转换 → 日程更易变化；固定工作或退休者可能较稳定 → 有限比较。 |
| [P259](#p259) | B | 生活经历与当前关切不同 → 可能选择不同书籍；兴趣也重叠 → 不绝对按年龄分。 |
| [P265](#p265) | B | 长者可能更关注固定社区，年轻人也关心影响自己的本地问题 → 地方联系和需要决定关注。 |
| [P274](#p274) | B | 儿童探索兴趣，成人兼顾收入和责任 → 目标内容与判断条件不同 → 不绝对化。 |
| [P275](#p275) | B | 学习或初入职业阶段的需要 → 技能、工作或独立生活目标 → 用软件发展素材。 |
| [P300](#p300) | B | 经历和生活需要不同 → 视频选择可能不同 → 兴趣重叠，不按年龄锁定类型。 |

<a id="p001"></a>

### P001 · B｜Which group pays more attention to health, young people or old people?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 年长者可能更有健康问题的切身体会 → 更重视日常习惯；年轻人也可能因运动目标关注健康。

**答案：**

> I'd expect older people to pay more attention to health, though there are plenty of exceptions.
> **Having experienced more health problems may make them take their daily habits more seriously.**
> For example, poor sleep might lead an older person to keep a regular bedtime, while a younger person still stays up late.
> So, I'd lean towards older people, but some young people are very health-conscious too.

**学习提示：** 比较是有限判断，不声称健康关注度的全国统计。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md) · [Describe a person whose health and fitness you have helped to improve](../part2/🧡🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20whose%20health%20and%20fitness%20you%20have%20helped%20to%20improve.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E39](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康)、[E40](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#3940-balanced-routines-and-health-均衡作息与健康) · [审计 P001](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p001)。

<a id="p057"></a>

### P057 · B｜What can young and old people learn from each other?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T01 学习、创造与执行

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q2

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)；辅助链：[AB01](#ab01)

**串法：** 年轻人与长者经验不同 → 可分别分享数字操作和生活经验 → 双向学习。

**答案：**

> Young and old people can learn different things from each other because they've had different experiences.
> Younger people may share **digital skills**, while older people may offer advice based on work or family life.
> For example, a student could help a grandparent use a messaging app, then ask how they handled a difficult conversation at work.
> So, both generations have useful things to share.

**学习提示：** 用 may 避免把数字能力或生活智慧按年龄固定划分。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages](❤️✅🥕🥕%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20is%20good%20at%20learning%20and%20speaking%20new%20languages.md) · [Describe someone who is older than you that you admire](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20who%20is%20older%20than%20you%20that%20you%20admire.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E6](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#6-generational-gap)、[E22](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#22-what-is-the-most-important-factor-in-learning-well) · [审计 P057](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p057)。

<a id="p059"></a>

### P059 · A｜Why is there a generation gap between the young and the old?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q1

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 经历、数字熟悉度与责任不同→需求习惯不同；再补变化限定。

**答案：**

> A generation gap often comes from growing up with different experiences and responsibilities.
> **Being more or less familiar with technology can also affect people's habits.**
> For example, a young person used to messaging apps may prefer texting, while an older relative unfamiliar with them prefers talking in person.
> So, different experiences can lead to different ways of doing things.

**学习提示：** 沿用既有 A 类短答案；Part 2 链接提供可替换场景，示意例子不新增本人经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe someone who is older than you that you admire](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20who%20is%20older%20than%20you%20that%20you%20admire.md) · [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md)

论点回查：[E5](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#5-older-vs-younger-people)、[E6](../../reviews/part3-opinion-practice-record.md#6-generational-gap) · [审计 P059](../../reviews/part3-transfer-audit.md#p059)。

<a id="p060"></a>

### P060 · B｜What do old people usually do in their daily life?

- [ ] 不看稿口述

主题：T09 年龄与代际 ＋ T02 健康、习惯与休闲

机制：M04 接触与熟悉度

题型：Q7

逻辑链：[本节 AB20 主链 / 对照](#ab20)

**串法：** 是否工作、照顾家庭及个人习惯不同 → 日常活动不同 → 列举时不把老人都当退休者。

**答案：**

> Older people may spend their days working, looking after family, exercising or meeting friends.
> **Their responsibilities and interests** shape their routine, and not everyone has retired.
> For example, one older person might have a morning job, while another takes a walk and then helps care for a grandchild.
> So, older people’s daily lives can vary quite a lot.

**学习提示：** 保留本题具体桥梁；人物、地点或任务的示意迁移不代表新增真实经历。

**Part 2 素材：** [Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden](../part2/%E2%9D%A4%EF%B8%8F%E2%9C%85%F0%9F%A5%95%20Describe%20a%20person%20who%20loves%20to%20grow%20plants%20%28e.g.%20vegetables%2C%20flowers%29%20at%20home%20or%20in%20the%20garden.md) · [Describe someone who is older than you that you admire](../part2/%F0%9F%A7%A1%20Describe%20someone%20who%20is%20older%20than%20you%20that%20you%20admire.md)
